# Get transactions from US users in the last 7 days
us_user_transactions = Users.WHERE(country == "US").CALCULATE(
    sent_transactions_last_7_days=transactions_sent.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
    ),
    received_transactions_last_7_days=transactions_received.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
    )
)

# Calculate the final result
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    num_transactions=COUNT(us_user_transactions.sent_transactions_last_7_days) + 
                    COUNT(us_user_transactions.received_transactions_last_7_days),
    total_amount=SUM(us_user_transactions.sent_transactions_last_7_days.amount) + 
                SUM(us_user_transactions.received_transactions_last_7_days.amount)
)

    (sending_user.country == "US")
)

    num_transactions=COUNT(us_user_transactions_last_7_days),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(us_user_transactions_last_7_days.amount)

# Filter user balances updated in the past week (inclusive of 7 days ago)
recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
)

# Calculate average balance per user for the past week
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(name="users", by=user_id).CALCULATE(
    user_avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Calculate the overall average of user average balances (AMB)
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(

[DEBUG] [Q98] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter UserBalances for records updated in the past week (inclusive of 7 days ago).
# DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7 includes records from 7 days ago up to the current timestamp.
recent_user_balances = Ewallet.UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered balances by user_id to calculate the Average Monthly Balance (AMB) per user.
# The 'user_id' is used as the partition key.
user_ambs_per_user = recent_user_balances.PARTITION(name="user_groups", by=user_id).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the average balance for each user within their respective group (their AMB)
    user_amb=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the overall average of these per-user AMBs.
# We access the 'user_amb' calculated in the previous step and take its average.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_amb_overall=AVG(user_ambs_per_user.user_amb)
)

[DEBUG] [Q102] Running models for: What is the PMDAU (Per Month Daily Active Users) for wallet transactions in the last 2 months excluding the current month?PMDAU (Per Month Daily Active Users) = distinct sender with  sender_type = 0. Truncate created_at to month for aggregation.
[DEBUG] [Q102] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q102] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by claude:
# Filter transactions for user senders in the last 2 months (excluding current month)
user_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 2)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_id=sender_id
)

# Group by month and calculate PMDAU (distinct senders per month)
result = user_transactions.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q102] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Determine the date range for the last two full months
# Get the start of the current month
current_month_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
# Get the start of the month two months ago
two_months_ago_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 month')

# Step 2: Filter transactions for the specified period and sender type (0 for users)
filtered_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (Ewallet.Transactions.created_at >= two_months_ago_start_date) &
    (Ewallet.Transactions.created_at < current_month_start_date) &
    (Ewallet.Transactions.sender_type == 0)
)

# Step 3: Prepare data for daily and monthly aggregation
# Extract the month and day from created_at and keep sender_id
transaction_details = filtered_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    transaction_day=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day'),
    sender_id=sender_id
)

# Step 4: Calculate Daily Active Users (DAU)
# Partition by month and day to group transactions for daily distinct sender count
daily_active_users = transaction_details.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups",
    by=(transaction_month, transaction_day)
).CALCULATE(
    month_value=transaction_month,
    day_value=transaction_day,
    # Count distinct sender_id for each day within the daily_groups partition
    daily_active_users_count=NDISTINCT(transaction_details.sender_id)
)

# Step 5: Calculate Per Month Daily Active Users (PMDAU)
# Partition the daily_active_users by month to sum up DAU for each month
result = daily_active_users.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=month_value
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_value,
    pmdau=SUM(monthly_groups.daily_active_users_count)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Step 1: Determine the date range for the last two full months
# Get the start of the current month
current_month_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
# Get the start of the month two months ago
two_months_ago_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 month')

# Step 2: Filter transactions for the specified period and sender type (0 for users)
filtered_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (Ewallet.Transactions.created_at >= two_months_ago_start_date) &
    (Ewallet.Transactions.created_at < current_month_start_date) &
    (Ewallet.Transactions.sender_type == 0)
)

# Step 3: Prepare data for daily and monthly aggregation
# Extract the month and day from created_at and keep sender_id
transaction_details = filtered_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    transaction_day=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day'),
    sender_id=sender_id
)

daily_active_users = transaction_details.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups",
    by=(transaction_month, transaction_day)
).CALCULATE(
    month_value=transaction_month,
    day_value=transaction_day,
    # Count distinct sender_id for each day within the daily_groups partition
    daily_active_users_count=NDISTINCT(transaction_details.sender_id)
)

[DEBUG] [Q107] Running models for: What was the STR for wallet transactions in the previous month?STR (success transaction rate) = number of successful transactions / total number of transactions
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by gemini:
# Calculate the start and end dates for the previous month
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
previous_month_start = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-1 month')
previous_month_end = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-1 second')

# Filter transactions for the previous month
transactions_previous_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= previous_month_start) & (created_at <= previous_month_end)
)

# Calculate the total number of transactions and successful transactions for the previous month
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_previous_month),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_previous_month.WHERE(status == 'success'))
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR)
    # Use IFF to handle division by zero if total_transactions is 0
    str_for_previous_month=IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    )
)
[DEBUG] [Q107] DataFrame from gemini is None

    (YEAR(created_at) == YEAR(DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))) &
    (MONTH(created_at) == MONTH(DATETIME('now', '-1 month')))
)

    total_transactions=COUNT(previous_month_transactions),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(previous_month_transactions.WHERE(
        (status == "completed") | (status == "success")
    )),
    str_rate=COUNT(previous_month_transactions.WHERE(
        (status == "completed") | (status == "success")
    )) * 100.0 / COUNT(previous_month_transactions)
)

    (created_at >= previous_month_start) & (created_at <= previous_month_end)

# Calculate the total number of transactions and successful transactions for the previous month
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_previous_month),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_previous_month.WHERE(status == 'success'))
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR)
    # Use IFF to handle division by zero if total_transactions is 0
    str_for_previous_month=IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by claude:
# Calculate STR for wallet transactions in the previous month
previous_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == YEAR(DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))) &
    (MONTH(created_at) == MONTH(DATETIME('now', '-1 month')))
)

str_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    total_transactions=COUNT(previous_month_transactions),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(previous_month_transactions.WHERE(
        (status == "completed") | (status == "success")
    )),
    str_rate=COUNT(previous_month_transactions.WHERE(
        (status == "completed") | (status == "success")
    )) * 100.0 / COUNT(previous_month_transactions)
)

# First, get merchant info with their creation month
merchant_info = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    merchant_name=name,
    merchant_creation_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(created_at), MONTH(created_at))
)

# Get coupon info with their creation month and link to merchant
coupon_info = merchant_info.coupons.CALCULATE(
    coupon_creation_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(created_at), MONTH(created_at))
)

# Filter coupons that were created in the same month as their merchant
same_month_coupons = coupon_info.WHERE(
    coupon_creation_month == merchant_creation_month
)

# Count coupons per merchant for the same creation month
merchant_coupon_counts = merchant_info.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=merchant_id,
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    num_coupons=COUNT(same_month_coupons)
)

# Find the merchant with the highest number of coupons
result = merchant_coupon_counts.TOP_K(1, by=num_coupons.DESC())

# Step 1: Calculate the creation year and month for each merchant
merchants_with_creation_info = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    merchant_name=name,
    merchant_creation_year=YEAR(created_at),
    merchant_creation_month=MONTH(created_at)
)

# Step 2: For each merchant, filter their coupons to include only those created
# in the same year and month as the merchant's creation date.
# Then, count these filtered coupons for each merchant.
merchant_coupon_counts = merchants_with_creation_info.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=merchant_id,
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    num_coupons=COUNT(
        coupons.WHERE(
            (YEAR(created_at) == merchant_creation_year) &
            (MONTH(created_at) == merchant_creation_month)
        )
    )
)

# Step 3: Find the merchant with the highest number of such coupons.
highest_coupon_merchant = merchant_coupon_counts.TOP_K(1, by=num_coupons.DESC())

# Step 4: Select the requested fields for the final result.
result = highest_coupon_merchant.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=merchant_id,
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    num_coupons=num_coupons
)

# Filter transactions for 2023, successful status, and sent by active users
user_transactions_2023 = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "successful") &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 represents users, not merchants
).CALCULATE(
    month_date = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_user_id = sender_id
)

# Group by month and count distinct active users who sent money
result = user_transactions_2023.PARTITION(name="months", by=month_date).CALCULATE(
    month_date = month_date,
    distinct_active_users = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.WHERE(status == "active").uid)
).ORDER_BY(month_date.ASC())
ERROR WHILE EXECUTING QUERY:
WITH _s2 AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT
    DATE(created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND sender_type = 1
    AND status = 'successful'
), _s3 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT users.uid) AS agg_0,
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily AS wallet_transactions_daily
  JOIN main.users AS users
    ON users.status = 'active' AND users.uid = wallet_transactions_daily.sender_id
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', wallet_transactions_daily.created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.sender_type = 1
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.status = 'successful'
  GROUP BY
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month')
)
SELECT
  _s2.month_date,
  COALESCE(_s3.agg_0, 0) AS distinct_active_users
FROM _s2 AS _s2
LEFT JOIN _s3 AS _s3
  ON _s2.month_date = _s3.month_date
ORDER BY
  month_date

[DEBUG] [Q110] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1 & 2: Filter transactions for 2023, successful status, and sent by a user.
# Then, calculate the 'transaction_month' (start of the month) and select the sender_id.
filtered_transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "successful") &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) # Ensures the sender is a user, not a merchant
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    user_id=sender_id # Renaming sender_id for clarity in the distinct count
)

# Step 3 & 4: Partition the filtered transactions by 'transaction_month'
# and then count the number of distinct 'user_id's within each month.
monthly_active_users = filtered_transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=transaction_month
).CALCULATE(
    month_date=transaction_month, # The month as a date
    num_active_users=NDISTINCT(monthly_groups.user_id) # Count distinct user IDs in each monthly group
)

# Step 5: Order the results by 'month_date' in ascending order.
result = monthly_active_users.ORDER_BY(month_date.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q118] Running models for: Which are the top 5 countries by total transaction amount sent by users, sender_type = 0? Return the country, number of distinct users who sent, and total transaction amount.
[DEBUG] [Q118] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q118] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q118] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only those sent by users (sender_type == 0)
# and calculate the sender's country, sender's UID, and the transaction amount.
user_transactions_data = Transactions.WHERE(sender_type == 0).CALCULATE(
    sender_country=sending_user.country,
    sender_uid=sending_user.uid,
    transaction_amount=amount
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by the sender's country.
# Within each country partition, calculate the total transaction amount
# and the number of distinct users who sent transactions.
country_transaction_summary = user_transactions_data.PARTITION(
    name="countries", by=sender_country
).CALCULATE(
    country=sender_country,
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(user_transactions_data.transaction_amount),
    num_distinct_users=NDISTINCT(user_transactions_data.sender_uid)
)

# Step 3: Select the top 5 countries based on the total transaction amount
# in descending order.
result = country_transaction_summary.TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

    (receiver_type == 1) &

).receiving_merchant.CALCULATE(

[DEBUG] [Q119] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions that are received by merchants (receiver_type == 1)
# and occurred within the last 150 days (inclusive).
recent_merchant_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (Ewallet.Transactions.receiver_type == 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", Ewallet.Transactions.created_at, 'now') <= 150)
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by the receiving merchant's ID.
# For each merchant, calculate the total transaction amount and the total number of transactions.
merchant_transaction_summary = recent_merchant_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="merchant_groups", by=recent_merchant_transactions.receiving_merchant.mid
).CALCULATE(
    # Retrieve the merchant's name from the singular 'receiving_merchant' relationship
    merchant_name=recent_merchant_transactions.receiving_merchant.name,
    # Calculate the sum of 'amount' for all transactions within this merchant group
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(recent_merchant_transactions.amount),
    # Count the number of transactions within this merchant group
    total_transactions=COUNT(recent_merchant_transactions)
)

# Step 3: Select the top 2 merchants based on their total transaction amount in descending order.
result = merchant_transaction_summary.TOP_K(
    2, by=merchant_transaction_summary.total_transaction_amount.DESC()
)
[DEBUG] [Q119] DataFrame from gemini is None

[DEBUG] [Q119] Running model gemini (attempt 1)
[DEBUG] [Q119] Code generated by claude:
result = Merchants.WHERE(
    HAS(transactions_received.WHERE(
        (receiver_type == 1) & 
        (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 150)
    )) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,

)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by the receiving merchant's ID.
# For each merchant, calculate the total transaction amount and the total number of transactions.
merchant_transaction_summary = recent_merchant_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="merchant_groups", by=recent_merchant_transactions.receiving_merchant.mid
).CALCULATE(
    # Retrieve the merchant's name from the singular 'receiving_merchant' relationship
    merchant_name=recent_merchant_transactions.receiving_merchant.name,
    # Calculate the sum of 'amount' for all transactions within this merchant group
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(recent_merchant_transactions.amount),
    # Count the number of transactions within this merchant group
    total_transactions=COUNT(recent_merchant_transactions)
)

result = merchant_transaction_summary.TOP_K(
    2, by=merchant_transaction_summary.total_transaction_amount.DESC()

# First, find the earliest coupon for each merchant
earliest_coupons = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(coupons) == 1).coupons.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
).SINGULAR()

    HAS(coupons) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    registration_date=created_at,
    earliest_coupon_id=earliest_coupons.cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=earliest_coupons.start_date
).WHERE(

[DEBUG] [Q123] DataFrame from claude is None
[DEBUG] [Q123] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Prepare merchant information, including their registration date.
merchant_info = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    registration_date=created_at
)

# Step 2: For each merchant, find their earliest coupon.
# We access the 'coupons' sub-collection and rank them by 'start_date' in ascending order.
# 'per="Merchants"' ensures the ranking is done within each merchant's coupons.
# We then filter for rank 1 to get the earliest coupon and use SINGULAR() as we expect only one earliest coupon.
earliest_coupon_details = merchant_info.coupons.CALCULATE(
    coupon_id=cid,
    coupon_start_date=start_date,
    coupon_rank=RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants")
).WHERE(
    coupon_rank == 1
).SINGULAR()

# Step 3: Filter the merchants based on the condition that the earliest coupon's start date
# is within one year of the merchant's registration date.
# The 'registration_date' is available here because it was down-streamed from 'merchant_info'.
filtered_merchants_with_coupons = earliest_coupon_details.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("years", registration_date, coupon_start_date) <= 1
)

# Step 4: Select and rename the final output fields as requested.
result = filtered_merchants_with_coupons.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=merchant_id,
    registration_date=registration_date,
    earliest_coupon_id=coupon_id,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=coupon_start_date
)
